T0提单时点模型V4｜模型说明与实测效果

面向业务介绍与技术沟通的完整说明

版本 t0_timing_v4_20261008 · 文档2026年10月26日 · 实测数据截至2026年10月24日

认识模型如何预测使用特征训练验证实测效果使用流程问题与依据

一、模型解决什么问题

在用户T0获额通过时，预测其首次提单发生的时间。模型使用获额前的借还款、产品和行为信息，输出D0、D3、D7、D15累计提单概率，用于低意愿候选筛选。
实际输入列1,930多窗口、统计量、交互和编码展开后的字段
共享LightGBM模型838棵树一个二分类器，四个预测区间共享
基础候选当天未提单命中85.97%3,143 / 3,656次可判候选
叠加410高分后的命中90.69%1,373 / 1,514次可判候选
可以筛选低意愿候选

历史实测有区分度，可以开展实时评分接入测试。

当前不可以直接全量自动发优惠

实时供数、评分时效和资格尚未验收。

不能认定优惠已提高提单和利润

本次检验没有随机优惠对照结果。

T0纳入：结清续贷获额通过；同日存在获额前使已放款在贷订单归零的结清，匹配最近一次结清。同用户相邻获额间隔≤20秒链式合并，保留链尾；不同T0事件分别计数。目标是用户后续首次实际提单，不限定同一订单号，不要求审核通过或放款。

二、模型如何计算

用一个LightGBM模型学习四个区间的条件首次提单概率，逐区间做sigmoid概率校准，再转换为累计概率。

D0获额时刻—当天结束

当天首次提单

D1—D3次日—第3日结束

此前未提单者在本段首次提单

D4—D7第4日—第7日结束

此前未提单者在本段首次提单

D8—D15第8日—第15日结束

此前未提单者在本段首次提单

累计提单概率＝1－截至该阶段各区间“未提单概率”的连乘
计算示例（非真实个体）：当天提单概率20%；当天未提单者在D1—D3提单的概率30%。因此pD3＝20%＋80%×30%＝44%。

输出关系：pD0 ≤ pD3 ≤ pD7 ≤ pD15。所有窗口使用America/Belize业务时区。

查看四个输出字段及概率定义
输出字段 / 文中简称预测目标观察窗口
p_apply_d0 / pD0当天累计获额时刻到当天结束
p_apply_d3 / pD3截至第3日累计包含D0—D3，共4个自然日期
p_apply_d7 / pD7截至第7日累计包含D0—D7，共8个自然日期
p_apply_d15 / pD15截至第15日累计包含D0—D15，共16个自然日期

pD3＜50%是短期低意愿候选规则，不等于预测第4日后一定提单。概率取0—1；0.35表示预测累计提单概率35%。

查看技术结构与参数
项目冻结事实
算法一个全局LightGBM LGBMClassifier，binary目标；838棵树
阶段输入interval_id、interval_length、interval_start_day，共3列
校准4个逐区间sigmoid校准器，基于历史样本外预测拟合
当前参数num_leaves=63；max_depth=8；learning_rate=0.025；min_child_samples=200；reg_lambda=10；reg_alpha=0.1
产品表达独立产品编码、未知产品/新组合和缺失标记；编码词表只用训练期拟合
实际模型global共享模型；未采用借款2—3次独立专家

算法术语参见LightGBM官方说明；校准概念参见scikit-learn官方概率校准文档。本模型的树数、字段与校准配置均由本地冻结文件核对。

三、使用哪些特征

借还款、额度及历史交互716列

借款次数、额度使用率、借还间隔、结清后再借、提前还款和额度变化。

历史T0提单习惯315列

过去获额后多久提单、历史各阶段提单率及近期变化。

历史循环贷经历216列

循环贷资格与提单经历、最近一次结果、连续未提单情况。

产品条件与选择218列

本次产品费用、利率、期限及相对上笔变化，历史产品选择。

App行为457列

活跃、进入提单页、金额操作、计划详情、确认等；含非还款日支路。

风控分及缺失标记5列

405、363模型分及缺失标记；不包含410分。

预测阶段编码3列

模型当前预测的区间编号、区间长度、区间起点。

合计1,930个实际输入列；同一个业务概念可展开成多个窗口、统计量或编码。字段保留不代表每列都有独立增量价值。

行为数据来源

wangchuanliang.event_track_record明细。冻结模型沿用其create_time / create_date口径，未使用event_track汇总表或新星途埋点表。

区分还款与借款行为

另建剔除实际还款日的行为支路。还款日按有效还款流水时间识别，降低还款进提单页对意愿判断的干扰。

查看行为窗口和信息截止规则
特征系列准确时间口径
基础beh_events_1d / 3d / 7dT0前滚动24 / 72 / 168小时，严格排除T0及之后
app_all_* / app_nonpay_*含T0当日的1 / 3 / 7 / 14 / 30 / 60 / 90个Belize自然日；当日只取T0之前记录
app_*_1d获额业务日零点至T0之前，不是过去滚动24小时
App可观察历史从max(2024-01-01，注册日)起的可观察历史，不代表注册前或源表缺失期间行为
历史提单率只用T0之前已成熟的历史获额观察窗口
字段可用性发生时间截止T0是代码约束；部分迟报、后补与风控分历史可得时间尚未完成线上核验
405、363是模型输入；410是评分后的外部筛选条件。410≥528来自此前全量T0有效分的第80百分位；基准中占20.17%，同分不拆。它是统计分组线，未被证明是最优投放门槛。
查询1,930个实际模型输入字段

查询仅展示字段定义，不含用户值。全部1,930列均匹配冻结开发字典；其中76列原释义较简略，本页保留原文。完整公式、时间约束见下载字典。

四、训练与检验如何进行

训练期母样本170,832次2026年1月1日—8月15日T0事件
实际参与拟合的事件167,292次另3,540次无可用观察区间，不参与拟合
区间训练记录358,661条每个事件按仍未提单的区间展开
时间滚动验证

6月、7月、8月滚动验证；训练与验证保留15天以上间隔。

选型与校准

累计Brier优先、AUC辅助；使用样本外预测拟合分段sigmoid校准。

冻结后再检验

版本t0_timing_v4_20261008保持冻结；本次10月检验不重训、不重校准。

标签处理：发生首次提单后，不再产生后续区间训练记录；下一次严格T0截断原观察窗口，无法判断的结果记未知，不填成未提单。

五、真实预测效果

主检验：10月9—21日8,866次评分事件，8,717次结果可判、149次未知。实际结果截至10月24日完整业务日；D0与D3主指标采用同一8,717次可判事件。

当天排序AUC0.8196同8,717次事件；不是81.96%准确率
截至D3排序AUC0.8028同8,717次事件
模型平均预测当天提单38.84%实际：3,441 / 8,717＝39.47%
模型平均预测D3累计提单56.44%实际：5,055 / 8,717＝57.99%

同一8,717次事件按pD0排序，最高与最低各取精确10%预算，同分边界按权重分摊。每档871.7是事件权重，不是人数。均值接近实际不等于每个分档都完全准确。

上图分子/分母依次为5,276/8,717、3,143/3,656、1,622/2,044、1,373/1,514。四组重叠，不能相加。

85.97%：名单准不准

3,143÷3,656；候选中当天确实未提单的比例。513次当天仍提单，占14.03%；45次未知不计入此分母。

41.74%：名单有多大

3,701÷8,866；完整候选占全部评分事件的比例，未知结果仍计入。

90.69%：叠加风险分后的命中

1,373÷1,514；完整候选1,532次，其中18次未知。完整名单比例1,532÷8,866＝17.28%。

59.57%：目标覆盖多少

3,143÷5,276；全部当天未提单事件中，模型覆盖3,143次，未覆盖2,133次。

可以确认：模型将当天未提单事件集中到候选名单。不能认定：85.97%是全体准确率或优惠转化率。基础候选D0—D3均未提单为2,488÷3,656＝68.05%，与当天未提单85.97%分别计算。
10-09—10-1185.23%799次可判候选
10-12—10-1885.84%2,111次可判候选
10-19—10-2385.45%1,313次可判候选

分批补充采用10月9—23日D0成熟数据，与主样本重叠。固定pD3＜50%门槛，单日命中80.74%—90.23%；短期有效不能证明长期稳定。

六、如何使用与验收

1　准备特征

按冻结字典汇总获额前数据；严格保留时间口径与缺失标记。

2　获额触发评分

补入本次条件，调用冻结模型，保存四个累计概率和生成时刻。

3　先验收实时链路

记录当时实际可得输入、源水位、评分时延及资格；已提单或关键源缺失不发优惠。

4　再做优惠对照

通过验收后按用户固定随机分组，分别核算提单增量、优惠成本、风险与成熟收益。

当前执行边界：可以做候选筛选与实时接入测试。实时数据、评分时效、资格验收前，不直接全量自动发优惠；本次没有部署生产服务或发放优惠。
查看评分输入输出与上线记录要求
内容要求
输入身份与锚点业务用户ID、20秒去重后的T0事件ID、America/Belize获额通过时点
模型输入按冻结工程生成的数值矩阵及产品/类别信息；保持字段、顺序、编码与缺失处理一致
实际概率输出p_apply_d0、p_apply_d3、p_apply_d7、p_apply_d15；范围0—1且单调不减
生产记录输入快照、源到达水位、模型版本/哈希、评分开始结束时间、用户是否已提单、资格与拟曝光时间
候选规则使用原始累计概率pD3＜0.5；410≥528是额外条件，不替代风控审批
策略评估固定优惠前的原T0锚点；同一用户固定组别，政策引发下一T0不重置实验起点

七、常见问题与复原依据

业务介绍时怎么说？

本模型在T0获额时，利用用户既有借还款与行为信息，预测首次提单时间；历史检验证实它能筛出当天不提单候选，实时链路通过验收后用于小流量优惠对照测试。

1,930列是否代表1,930种信息？

不是。它包含同一业务概念的多个时间窗、统计量、交互、产品编码和三个阶段输入。字段数量不等于独立信息量。

可以把410说成模型内部特征吗？

不可以。内部风控分是405和363，410用于评分后的外部分组。410≥528定义此前基准中的最高约20%分组，不是已证最优收益阈值。

能把数据换成新星途埋点直接评分吗？

不能直接替换。冻结模型使用event_track_record及原窗口；换源须重新验证字段含义、分布、可得时点和评分效果。

85.97%能保证以后每批都达到吗？

不能。它是本批真实历史候选的命中率；逐日有波动，线上数据可得性与时延尚未完成验收。

模型能证明降息提额提高利润吗？

不能。当前是提单时点预测模型，不是优惠增量收益模型；必须通过真实随机对照核算。

下载代码与统计依据 ZIP 打开原业务检验报告

本说明独立生成；原验证报告保留。没有改变模型、统计样本或筛选规则。模型文件SHA256：57b2aac902044a78679872fb6120afdda906796705a35bbb867c9a757cc8d3ce。

原验证复原/code/t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/00_release_input_tables.sql
/* 仅在原授权数据环境创建并填入；此发布包不提供实际业务ID。
   batch_key必须与发布说明/manifest一致，不能把全部用户填入替代冻结名单。
   必须检查每批distinct数量、主键唯一性与规范化SHA256；空表不运行全量。
   三表与业务查询在同一连接可见；本脚本不自动取数、不自动填数。 */
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_users (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL
);
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_offers (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    credit_serial_id bigint NOT NULL
);
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_events (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    event_id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    as_of_time timestamp NOT NULL
);
原验证复原/code/t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/prepare_release_sql.py
"""Create publication SQL copies without embedded frozen business identifiers.

Original SQL is read-only. Target identifiers remain only in the authorized
environment; release artifacts carry scoped batch hashes/counts, not identifiers.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,re,zipfile
from collections import defaultdict

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
WORK=ROOT.parent
DEST=ROOT/'release_sql'
OUT=ROOT.parents[1]/'outputs/T0提单意愿模型V4_业务实测与上线建议_20261026'
ZIP=OUT/'T0模型V4业务实测_完整代码与统计依据.zip'


def digest_bytes(x):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
def digest_file(p):return digest_bytes(p.read_bytes())
def write_json(path,obj):path.write_text(json.dumps(obj,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP) as z:
        names=[n[len('code/'):] for n in z.namelist() if n.startswith('code/') and n.endswith('.sql')]
    groups={};records=[]
    in_pattern=re.compile(r'(?P<field>\b(?:\w+\.)?(?:user_id|credit_serial_id|serial_id))\s+IN\s*\((?P<numbers>[\d\s,\-]+)\)',re.I)
    values_pattern=re.compile(r"(?P<head>\bWITH\s+target_events\s*\(\s*event_id\s*,\s*user_id\s*,\s*as_of_time\s*\)\s+AS\s*\()\s*VALUES\s*(?P<rows>(?:\(\s*\d+::bigint\s*,\s*\d+::bigint\s*,\s*TIMESTAMP\s*'[^']+'\s*\)\s*,?\s*)+)\)",re.I)
    tuple_pattern=re.compile(r"\(\s*(\d+)::bigint\s*,\s*(\d+)::bigint\s*,\s*TIMESTAMP\s*'([^']+)'\s*\)",re.I)
    for relative in sorted(names):
        source=WORK/relative
        original=source.read_text()
        needed=[];replacements=[]
        def register(kind,canonical,n,unique,source_columns):
            h=digest_bytes(canonical.encode());key=kind+'_'+h[:16]
            spec={'batch_key':key,'input_kind':kind,'sha256':h,'canonical_count':n,'distinct_primary_key_count':unique,
                  'source_columns':source_columns,'files':[]}
            if key not in groups:groups[key]=spec
            assert groups[key]['sha256']==h
            groups[key]['files'].append(relative)
            if key not in needed:needed.append(key)
            return key
        def replace_in(match):
            field=match.group('field');nums=[int(x) for x in re.findall(r'-?\d+',match.group('numbers'))]
            if len(nums)<3 and not any(abs(x)>=100000 for x in nums):return match.group(0)
            vals=sorted(set(nums));kind='users' if field.lower().split('.')[-1]=='user_id' else 'offers'
            canonical=','.join(map(str,vals))
            key=register(kind,canonical,len(nums),len(vals),['user_id'] if kind=='users' else ['credit_serial_id'])
            column='user_id' if kind=='users' else 'credit_serial_id'
            replacements.append({'type':'identifier_IN_list','field':field,'batch_key':key,'original_literal_count':len(nums),'original_distinct_count':len(vals)})
            return f"{field} IN (SELECT {column} FROM validation_target_{kind} WHERE batch_key = '{key}')"
        changed=in_pattern.sub(replace_in,original)
        def replace_values(match):
            vals=[(int(a),int(b),c) for a,b,c in tuple_pattern.findall(match.group('rows'))]
            if not vals:raise ValueError('Unparsed target event VALUES')
            if len({x[0] for x in vals})!=len(vals):raise ValueError('Duplicate target event IDs in frozen SQL input')
            # Canonical timestamps are exactly the original SQL timestamp strings;
            # sorting makes the input hash independent of the VALUES row order.
            canonical=json.dumps(sorted(vals),ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))
            key=register('events',canonical,len(vals),len(vals),['event_id','user_id','as_of_time'])
            replacements.append({'type':'target_events_VALUES','batch_key':key,'original_literal_count':len(vals),'original_distinct_count':len(vals)})
            return match.group('head')+f"SELECT event_id,user_id,as_of_time FROM validation_target_events WHERE batch_key = '{key}')"
        changed=values_pattern.sub(replace_values,changed)
        if re.search(r'\bVALUES\b',changed,re.I):raise ValueError(f'Unprocessed VALUES remaining: {relative}')
        for m in in_pattern.finditer(changed):
            nums=re.findall(r'-?\d+',m.group('numbers'))
            if len(nums)>=3 or any(abs(int(x))>=100000 for x in nums):raise ValueError('Unprocessed target numeric IN list')
        header='/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。\n'
        header+=' * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。\n'
        for key in needed:header+=' * 输入批次：'+key+'\n'
        header+=' * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。\n */\n'
        if needed:
            guards=[]
            for key in needed:
                table='validation_target_'+groups[key]['input_kind']
                guards.append(f"EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {table} WHERE batch_key = '{key}')")
            if re.search(r'^\s*(?:SET|DROP|CREATE|BEGIN)\b',changed,re.I):raise ValueError('Unexpected non-query source needing manual wrapping')
            # Guarded outer SELECT changes nothing when the required batch is
            # loaded; missing input cannot expose an unrelated whole cohort.
            body="SELECT release_result.*\nFROM (\n"+changed.strip().rstrip(';')+"\n) release_result\nWHERE "+'\n  AND '.join(guards)+';\n'
        else:body=changed
        target=DEST/relative;target.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);target.write_text(header+body,encoding='utf-8')
        records.append({'source':str(source),'source_relative':relative,'release_path':str(target),'release_relative':str(target.relative_to(ROOT)),
          'source_sha256':digest_file(source),'release_sha256':digest_file(target),'required_batches':needed,'replacements':replacements,
          'source_unchanged_verified':digest_file(source)==digest_bytes(original.encode()),'empty_input_guard':bool(needed)})
    for g in groups.values():g['files']=sorted(set(g['files']))
    ddl="""/* 仅在原授权数据环境创建并填入；此发布包不提供实际业务ID。
   batch_key必须与发布说明/manifest一致，不能把全部用户填入替代冻结名单。
   必须检查每批distinct数量、主键唯一性与规范化SHA256；空表不运行全量。
   三表与业务查询在同一连接可见；本脚本不自动取数、不自动填数。 */
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_users (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL
);
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_offers (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    credit_serial_id bigint NOT NULL
);
CREATE TEMP TABLE validation_target_events (
    batch_key text NOT NULL,
    event_id bigint NOT NULL,
    user_id bigint NOT NULL,
    as_of_time timestamp NOT NULL
);
"""
    (DEST/'00_release_input_tables.sql').write_text(ddl,encoding='utf-8')
    instructions=['发布SQL输入表说明','',
      '用途：发布副本保留原查询逻辑与日期窗口，但移除实际业务用户ID、订单ID和逐事件锚点VALUES清单。原工作SQL未修改。',
      '查询不会默认扩展为全量。依赖输入的查询外层设有批次非空保护：未装载对应batch_key时返回空结果。',
      '复现前必须在原授权数据环境取得同一范围的冻结输入；本发布包不含这些清单。仅用当前全量用户或另一个批次替代，不等价于原检验。',
      '', '一、三类输入表',
      'validation_target_users(batch_key,user_id)：原user_id数字IN集合；同一用户批次按对应冻结范围装载。',
      'validation_target_offers(batch_key,credit_serial_id)：原T0/风险核验订单编号集合；serial_id过滤也读取credit_serial_id列，语义为原获额订单编号。',
      'validation_target_events(batch_key,event_id,user_id,as_of_time)：原target_events逐事件输入，保留原事件ID、用户ID和America/Belize本地锚点时间。',
      '可使用00_release_input_tables.sql创建会话临时输入表。装载方式由授权环境决定；本发布脚本不会自动查询或填充身份名单。',
      '', '二、各批次必须独立装载与验证',
      '下列仅给出数量与SHA256，不包含实际业务标识。8676、14134、18506用户集合是不同来源范围，不能混为一批。',
      'users/offers哈希规则：取原集合去重后按整数升序，十进制无前导零，用英文逗号连接，无空格或换行，UTF-8后计算SHA256。',
      'events哈希规则：按(event_id,user_id,as_of_time原SQL字符串)三元组排序，以JSON二维数组紧凑编码（separators=(逗号,冒号)，无额外空格），UTF-8后计算SHA256；时间字符串须保留原SQL精度/格式，不自行改时区。',
      '校验每批记录数与distinct主键数一致。events的event_id必须唯一；不能把同一事件重复装载或换成未来时刻。',
      '数量或哈希不一致即停止复现并核查冻结输入，不可通过删掉WHERE或扩大为全量绕过。',
      '示例原VALUES也统一输入化；其数量很小的独立批次仅为调用示例，不可与真实历史锚点混用。', '']
    for g in sorted(groups.values(),key=lambda x:(x['input_kind'],x['batch_key'])):
        instructions +=[f"批次：{g['batch_key']}",f"类型：{g['input_kind']}；原行数：{g['canonical_count']}；distinct主键数：{g['distinct_primary_key_count']}",f"SHA256：{g['sha256']}",'使用文件：'+', '.join(g['files']),'']
    instructions +=['三、执行与解释','先按文件清单装载正确批次，再核验数量/哈希，最后在同一授权环境执行对应发布SQL。',
      '无输入依赖的SQL保持原日期/业务条件；不是新增了身份筛选，也没有移除原有过滤。',
      '带输入保护的汇总查询在输入为空时也返回空，而不是输出零计数或其他无关全体汇总；正确装载时保持原业务逻辑。',
      '输入表名未附业务schema；需保证同一会话/搜索路径下可见。生产调度如改成持久输入表，必须继续携带batch_key与相同权限边界。',
      '发布副本未在数据库执行，本次只进行静态参数替换、清单数量/哈希与残留检查；不能把静态检查当数据库语法或运行性能验收。']
    (DEST/'00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt').write_text('\n'.join(instructions)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    manifest={'source_zip_inventory':str(ZIP),'sql_files':len(records),'input_batches':len(groups),'files':records,'batch_scopes':sorted(groups.values(),key=lambda x:x['batch_key']),
      'raw_identifiers_exported':False,'source_sql_modified':False,'database_execution_performed':False}
    write_json(ROOT/'release_sql_manifest.json',manifest)
    qa={'status':'passed_static','sql_files':len(records),'parameterized_files':sum(bool(x['replacements']) for x in records),'input_batches':len(groups),
      'batch_counts':[{'batch_key':g['batch_key'],'kind':g['input_kind'],'distinct_count':g['distinct_primary_key_count']} for g in groups.values()],
      'original_hashes_unchanged':all(x['source_unchanged_verified'] for x in records),
      'remaining_business_identifier_IN_lists':0,'remaining_target_event_VALUES_lists':0,
      'all_input_dependent_files_guarded_when_empty':all(x['empty_input_guard'] for x in records if x['required_batches']),
      'database_syntax_execution':'Not executed; static only','notes':'Original per-file SQL conditions retained except list-to-scoped-input substitution and explicit empty-input outer guard.'}
    write_json(ROOT/'release_sql_qa.json',qa)
    print(json.dumps(qa,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))


if __name__=='__main__':main()
原验证复原/code/t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/analyze_business_reliability.py
"""Business-facing reliability replay of frozen T0 V4, no DB and no training.

Prespecified thresholds and fixed same-volume ranking are descriptive diagnostics.
Actual applications are observed under historical policy, not untreated potential
outcomes. Unknown labels stay unknown; candidate counts never require outcomes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
os.environ.setdefault('OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS','2')
os.environ.setdefault('OMP_NUM_THREADS','2')
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
V=ROOT.parent/'t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025'
SEED=20261025
B=500


def clean(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict):return {str(k):clean(v) for k,v in x.items()}
    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple)):return [clean(v) for v in x]
    if isinstance(x,np.integer):return int(x)
    if isinstance(x,(float,np.floating)):return float(x) if np.isfinite(x) else None
    if isinstance(x,(pd.Timestamp,np.datetime64)):return str(x)
    return x


def ratio(a,b):
    a,b=np.asarray(a,float),np.asarray(b,float)
    return np.divide(a,b,out=np.full_like(b,np.nan),where=b>0)


def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def ncount(x):
    f=float(x)
    return int(round(f)) if abs(f-round(f))<1e-8 else f


def make_rules(common):
    rules=[{'rule':f'pD0_lt_{i/10:.1f}','score':'p_v4_d0','threshold':i/10,'kind':'probability_threshold'} for i in range(1,6)]
    rules +=[{'rule':f'pD3_lt_{i/10:.1f}','score':'p_v4_d3','threshold':i/10,'kind':'probability_threshold'} for i in range(2,6)]
    n=int((common.p_v4_d3<.5).sum());p=common.p_v4_d0.to_numpy()
    boundary=float(np.partition(p,n-1)[n-1]);less=int((p<boundary).sum());ties=int((p==boundary).sum())
    rules.append({'rule':'low_pD0_same_volume_as_pD3_lt_0.5','score':'p_v4_d0','threshold':boundary,
       'kind':'same_volume_fixed_boundary','boundary_tie_fraction':float((n-less)/ties),
       'same_volume_events_common_cohort':n,'boundary_tie_events_common':ties,
       'definition':'Boundary fitted only to equal pD3<0.5 event count in common8717; equal score ties get equal expected quota. Same boundary/fraction then applied unchanged to full8866 and supplemental dates.'})
    return rules


def selection(d,r):
    p=d[r['score']].to_numpy(float);t=r['threshold']
    s=(p<t).astype(float)
    if r['kind']=='same_volume_fixed_boundary':s[p==t]=r['boundary_tie_fraction']
    return s


def vectors(d,s):
    y0=d.y_d0.to_numpy(float);y3=d.y_d3.to_numpy(float)
    k0=np.isfinite(y0);k3=np.isfinite(y3)
    return {
      'full_scored_events':np.ones(len(d)),
      'cohort_d0_known':k0,'cohort_d0_unknown':~k0,'cohort_no_d0':y0==0,'cohort_d0_apply':y0==1,
      'cohort_d3_known':k3,'cohort_d3_unknown':~k3,'cohort_no_d3':y3==0,'cohort_d3_apply':y3==1,
      'all_candidates':s,'known_d0':s*k0,'unknown_d0':s*~k0,
      'no_d0':s*(y0==0),'d0_apply':s*(y0==1),
      'd3_known':s*k3,'d3_unknown':s*~k3,'d3_noapply':s*(y3==0),'d3_apply':s*(y3==1),
      'd1_d3_apply':s*(y0==0)*(y3==1),
      'd0_classifier_tn':s*(y0==0),'d0_classifier_fn':s*(y0==1),
      'd0_classifier_fp':(1-s)*(y0==0),'d0_classifier_tp':(1-s)*(y0==1),
      'd3_classifier_tn':s*(y3==0),'d3_classifier_fn':s*(y3==1),
      'd3_classifier_fp':(1-s)*(y3==0),'d3_classifier_tp':(1-s)*(y3==1),
    }


def metrics(c):
    pairs={
      'full_candidate_coverage':('all_candidates','full_scored_events'),
      'evaluable_candidate_coverage':('known_d0','cohort_d0_known'),
      'ppv_no_d0':('no_d0','known_d0'),
      'd0_apply_fraction_known_candidates':('d0_apply','known_d0'),
      'npv_d3':('d3_noapply','d3_known'),
      'd1_d3_apply_fraction_known_d3_candidates':('d1_d3_apply','d3_known'),
      'd0_nonapply_capture':('no_d0','cohort_no_d0'),
      'baseline_no_d0':('cohort_no_d0','cohort_d0_known'),
      'baseline_no_d3':('cohort_no_d3','cohort_d3_known'),
    }
    m={k:ratio(c[a],c[b]) for k,(a,b) in pairs.items()}
    m['observed_d0_apply_per_100_known_candidates']=100*m['d0_apply_fraction_known_candidates']
    m['ppv_no_d0_lift_vs_baseline_pp']=100*(m['ppv_no_d0']-m['baseline_no_d0'])
    m['d0_classification_accuracy']=ratio(c['d0_classifier_tn']+c['d0_classifier_tp'],c['cohort_d0_known'])
    m['d3_classification_accuracy']=ratio(c['d3_classifier_tn']+c['d3_classifier_tp'],c['cohort_d3_known'])
    m['d0_majority_class_accuracy_baseline']=np.maximum(m['baseline_no_d0'],1-m['baseline_no_d0'])
    m['d3_majority_class_accuracy_baseline']=np.maximum(m['baseline_no_d3'],1-m['baseline_no_d3'])
    # Worst/best-case identification bounds, distinct from statistical CIs.
    m['all_candidate_no_d0_identification_lower']=ratio(c['no_d0'],c['all_candidates'])
    m['all_candidate_no_d0_identification_upper']=ratio(c['no_d0']+c['unknown_d0'],c['all_candidates'])
    m['all_candidate_d0_apply_per_100_identification_lower']=100*ratio(c['d0_apply'],c['all_candidates'])
    m['all_candidate_d0_apply_per_100_identification_upper']=100*ratio(c['d0_apply']+c['unknown_d0'],c['all_candidates'])
    return m


def evaluate(d,rules,cohort_name):
    codes,users=pd.factorize(d.user_id,sort=True);u=len(users)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED);boot=rng.multinomial(u,np.full(u,1/u),size=B)
    result=[];resampled={}
    for r in rules:
        s=selection(d,r);vs=vectors(d,s);keys=list(vs)
        point_counts={k:float(np.sum(vs[k])) for k in keys};point=metrics(point_counts)
        user_matrix=np.column_stack([np.bincount(codes,weights=vs[k],minlength=u) for k in keys])
        z=boot@user_matrix;bs=metrics({k:z[:,i] for i,k in enumerate(keys)})
        row={'cohort':cohort_name,**r,**{k:ncount(v) for k,v in point_counts.items()},
          'full_scored_users':u,'all_candidate_users':int(d.loc[s>0,'user_id'].nunique()),
          'known_candidate_users':int(d.loc[(s>0)&d.y_d0.notna().to_numpy(),'user_id'].nunique()),
          'candidate_users_count_status':'Exact' if set(np.unique(s)).issubset({0,1}) else 'Users with positive tie quota; event counts use expected allocation',
          **{k:float(v) for k,v in point.items()}}
        for k,v in bs.items():
            finite=np.asarray(v)[np.isfinite(v)]
            row[k+'_ci95_low']=float(np.quantile(finite,.025)) if len(finite) else None
            row[k+'_ci95_high']=float(np.quantile(finite,.975)) if len(finite) else None
            row[k+'_bootstrap_valid']=len(finite)
        row['sparse_zero_observed_d0_error_warning']=bool(point_counts['known_d0']>0 and point_counts['d0_apply']==0)
        result.append(row);resampled[r['rule']]=bs
    return result,resampled


def flows(d,name):
    return {'cohort':name,'events':len(d),'users':int(d.user_id.nunique()),
      **{f'{h}_mature':int(d[f'mature_{h}'].sum()) for h in ['d0','d3','d7']},
      **{f'{h}_known':int(d[f'y_{h}'].notna().sum()) for h in ['d0','d3','d7']},
      **{f'{h}_unknown':int(d[f'y_{h}'].isna().sum()) for h in ['d0','d3','d7']},
      'date_start':str(d.offer_date.min()),'date_end':str(d.offer_date.max())}


def d7_composition(d):
    z=d[d.p_v4_d3<.5].copy()
    y0=z.y_d0.to_numpy(float);y3=z.y_d3.to_numpy(float);y7=z.y_d7.to_numpy(float)
    categories={
      'D0':y0==1,
      'D1_D3':(y0==0)&(y3==1),
      'D4_D7':(y3==0)&(y7==1),
      'NO_APPLY_THROUGH_D7':y7==0,
      'UNKNOWN':~np.isfinite(y7),
    }
    assert np.array(list(categories.values())).sum(axis=0).min()==1
    assert np.array(list(categories.values())).sum(axis=0).max()==1
    codes,users=pd.factorize(z.user_id,sort=True);u=len(users);rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    boots=rng.multinomial(u,np.full(u,1/u),size=B)
    total_bs=boots@np.bincount(codes,minlength=u)
    known_bs=boots@np.bincount(codes,weights=np.isfinite(y7),minlength=u)
    rows=[]
    for key,mask in categories.items():
        n=int(mask.sum());num_bs=boots@np.bincount(codes,weights=mask,minlength=u)
        allshares=ratio(num_bs,total_bs);knownshares=ratio(num_bs,known_bs)
        row={'bucket':key,'events':n,'users':int(z.loc[mask,'user_id'].nunique()),
          'all_candidates':len(z),'known_d7_candidates':int(np.isfinite(y7).sum()),
          'share_all_candidates':n/len(z),'share_all_candidates_ci95_low':float(np.nanquantile(allshares,.025)),
          'share_all_candidates_ci95_high':float(np.nanquantile(allshares,.975)),
          'share_known_d7_candidates':n/np.isfinite(y7).sum() if key!='UNKNOWN' else None,
          'share_known_d7_candidates_ci95_low':float(np.nanquantile(knownshares,.025)) if key!='UNKNOWN' else None,
          'share_known_d7_candidates_ci95_high':float(np.nanquantile(knownshares,.975)) if key!='UNKNOWN' else None}
        rows.append(row)
    nlate=int(categories['D4_D7'].sum());n3zero=int((y3==0).sum());unknownafter3=int(((y3==0)&~np.isfinite(y7)).sum())
    return {'cohort_flow':flows(d,'D7_mature_20261009_20261017'),'selected_candidates':len(z),
      'selected_users':u,'known_d7_candidates':int(np.isfinite(y7).sum()),'unknown_d7_candidates':int((~np.isfinite(y7)).sum()),
      'rows':rows,'actual_d3_nonapply_candidates':n3zero,'actual_d3_nonapply_then_d4_7_apply':nlate,
      'actual_d3_nonapply_d7_unknown':unknownafter3,
      'd4_7_apply_share_of_actual_d3_nonapply_known_d7':nlate/(n3zero-unknownafter3) if n3zero>unknownafter3 else None,
      'interpretation':'Low predicted D3 application selects non-application propensity through D3; it does not predict that these people will apply after D3. D4-D7 actual application, D7 non-application and unknown remain separate.'}


def main():
    input_pred=V/'events_predictions.parquet';input_labels=V/'evaluation/labels_evaluated.parquet'
    p=pd.read_parquet(input_pred);d=pd.read_parquet(input_labels)
    a=p.set_index('event_id').sort_index();b=d.set_index('event_id').sort_index()
    for k in ['user_id','as_of_time','p_v4_d0','p_v4_d3']:np.testing.assert_equal(a[k].to_numpy(),b[k].to_numpy())
    full=d[(d.offer_date>='2026-10-09')&(d.offer_date<='2026-10-21')].copy()
    common=full[full.mature_d3&full.y_d3.notna()].copy()
    quality=d[(d.offer_date>='2026-10-09')&(d.offer_date<='2026-10-23')].copy()
    d7=d[(d.offer_date>='2026-10-09')&(d.offer_date<='2026-10-17')].copy()
    assert len(full)==8866 and len(common)==8717
    assert quality.mature_d0.all() and d7.mature_d7.all()
    rules=make_rules(common)
    rows_main,bs=evaluate(common,rules,'common_D3_mature_known_8717')
    rows_full,_=evaluate(full,rules,'full_D3_mature_scored_8866')
    rows_quality,_=evaluate(quality,rules,'D0_mature_exclude_oct24_20261009_20261023')
    # Main fairness and complete-candidate disclosure side by side, never swap denominators.
    full_lookup={x['rule']:x for x in rows_full}
    for r in rows_main:
        r['complete_scored_event_count']=len(full)
        r['complete_all_candidates']=full_lookup[r['rule']]['all_candidates']
        r['complete_all_candidate_users']=full_lookup[r['rule']]['all_candidate_users']
        r['complete_unknown_d0']=full_lookup[r['rule']]['unknown_d0']
        r['complete_candidate_coverage']=full_lookup[r['rule']]['full_candidate_coverage']
        assert abs(r['known_d0']-full_lookup[r['rule']]['known_d0'])<1e-8
    primary=next(x for x in rows_main if x['rule']=='pD3_lt_0.5')
    assert primary['all_candidates']==3656 and primary['no_d0']==3143 and primary['d0_apply']==513
    pairs=[];anchor='pD3_lt_0.5';lookup={x['rule']:x for x in rows_main}
    for rule in ['pD0_lt_0.5','low_pD0_same_volume_as_pD3_lt_0.5']:
        for metric in ['ppv_no_d0','observed_d0_apply_per_100_known_candidates','d0_classification_accuracy','npv_d3']:
            delta=bs[rule][metric]-bs[anchor][metric]
            multiplier=1 if metric.startswith('observed_') else 100
            pairs.append({'rule':rule,'reference':anchor,'metric':metric,
              'difference_rule_minus_reference_pp_or_per100':multiplier*(lookup[rule][metric]-lookup[anchor][metric]),
              'paired_ci95_low':float(np.nanquantile(multiplier*delta,.025)),
              'paired_ci95_high':float(np.nanquantile(multiplier*delta,.975)),
              'unit':'per100_known_candidates' if multiplier==1 else 'percentage_points'})
    daily=[];weekly=[]
    consistency_rules=[r for r in rules if r['kind']=='probability_threshold']
    for date,z in quality.groupby('offer_date',sort=True):
        result,_=evaluate(z,consistency_rules,'daily_D0_mature_quality')
        for r in result:r['date']=str(date.date());r['D3_mature_date']=bool(z.mature_d3.all())
        daily.extend(result)
    for week,z in quality.groupby('offer_week',sort=True):
        result,_=evaluate(z,consistency_rules,'weekly_D0_mature_quality')
        for r in result:r['week_start']=str(week);r['observed_date_start']=str(z.offer_date.min().date());r['observed_date_end']=str(z.offer_date.max().date());r['D3_fully_mature_week']=bool(z.mature_d3.all())
        weekly.extend(result)
    comp=d7_composition(d7)
    old=json.loads((ROOT.parent/'t0_offer_strategy_20261025/model_targeting.json').read_text())['headlines_at_threshold_0_5']['main_d3_mature_20261009_20261021']
    assert abs(primary['ppv_no_d0']-old['ppv_no_d0'])<1e-12
    assert abs(primary['ppv_no_d0_ci95_low']-old['ppv_no_d0_ci95_low'])<1e-12
    assert abs(primary['ppv_no_d0_ci95_high']-old['ppv_no_d0_ci95_high'])<1e-12
    result={
      'meta':{
        'model':'Frozen T0 application timing V4; no retraining/calibration/database query',
        'source_outcome_cutoff':'2026-10-25 00:00:00 America/Belize exclusive; outcomes through October24',
        'main':'Same8,717 D3-mature, label-known events dated October9-21 for all fair comparisons; complete8,866 scored events including149 unknown outcomes shown separately.',
        'supplement':'October9-23 D0-mature events, excluding all October24 scores because that day lacked risk source matches in prior audit. This exclusion does not prove every other historical feature was contemporaneously available.',
        'D0':'After passed T0 offer timestamp until next Belize midnight, not rolling24hours',
        'D3':'D0 through day3 end, event_date+4days exclusive; four natural dates, not rolling72hours',
        'D7':'D0 through day7 end, event_date+8days exclusive; main D7 cohort October9-17 only',
        'outcome':'First actual user application before next strict T0; not successful disbursement',
        'negative_prediction_rule':'p<cutoff; equality predicts application. Fixed probability grids are descriptive, not selected from these results for rollout.',
        'same_volume':'Exact3,656 expected event quota on common8,717 by ascending pD0. Equal-score boundary allocated equally; fixed boundary/fraction applied elsewhere. Not necessarily the same complete-candidate volume or supplemental coverage.',
        'main_accuracy':'ppv_no_d0 is actual D0 non-application among known selected candidates (NPV for application label). It is not full-population classification accuracy or incentive uplift.',
        'per100':'observed_d0_apply_per_100_known_candidates is100*(actual D0 applications / known candidates). These observed historical applications are a potential offer-leakage proxy, not a measured causal wasted-incentive rate.',
        'unknown':'Unknown/censored and administratively immature windows remain unknown. Full-candidate identification bounds are distinct from95% bootstrap confidence intervals.',
        'bootstrap':f'{B} percentile user-cluster replicates, seed{SEED}; repeated user events resampled together, paired rules share the same draws. Daily/weekly CIs are descriptive with no multiplicity-adjusted stability guarantee.',
        'daily_weekly':'Fixed gates on all D0-mature October9-23 dates; D3 metrics on later dates only use mature/known subsets and can be unavailable. Partial boundary weeks explicitly dated.',
        'classification':'Classification accuracy assumes selected candidates predict no application and all others predict application, separately measured for D0 and D3 known labels. Always-majority accuracy supplied; pD3 rule judged against D0 is a decision proxy, not a trained D0 classifier.',
        'evidence':'Retrospective replay, not verified contemporaneous online scoring, randomized incentive response, profitability proof, or production approval.',
      },
      'source_files':{str(input_pred):sha(input_pred),str(input_labels):sha(input_labels)},
      'rule_definitions':rules,
      'cohort_flow':[flows(full,'full8866'),flows(common,'common8717'),flows(quality,'D0_quality_supplement'),flows(d7,'D7_mature')],
      'rules_main':rows_main,'rules_complete_scored':rows_full,'rules_d0_quality_supplement':rows_quality,
      'paired_comparison':pairs,'consistency_daily':daily,'consistency_weekly':weekly,
      'd7_outcome_composition':comp,
      'reproduced_prior_main_85_97_and_interval':True,
    }
    for fname,rows in [('business_reliability_rules_main.csv',rows_main),('business_reliability_rules_full.csv',rows_full),('business_reliability_rules_d0_quality.csv',rows_quality),('business_reliability_daily.csv',daily),('business_reliability_weekly.csv',weekly),('business_reliability_pairs.csv',pairs),('business_reliability_d7_composition.csv',comp['rows'])]:
        pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(ROOT/fname,index=False)
    (ROOT/'business_reliability_metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(clean(result),ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(clean({'rules_main':len(rows_main),'daily':len(daily),'weekly':len(weekly),'primary_pD3_0_5':primary,'D7_composition':comp}),ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':main()
原验证复原/code/t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/make_business_examples.py
"""Business examples from frozen validation / existing profit files only.

No database connection, no retraining, no financial refresh. Decile mass uses
the original evaluator's fair fractional boundary / exact-score-tie weights.
Public anonymous cases are picked by fixed category then earliest T0.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
W=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
V=W.parent/'t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025'
R=W.parent/'t0_risk410_lifecycle_strategy_20261025'
sys.path.insert(0,str(V))
from evaluate_validation import SortedMetrics

def clean(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict):return {str(k):clean(v) for k,v in x.items()}
    if isinstance(x,(tuple,list)):return [clean(v) for v in x]
    if isinstance(x,np.integer):return int(x)
    if isinstance(x,(float,np.floating)):return float(x) if np.isfinite(x) else None
    if x is pd.NaT:return None
    if isinstance(x,pd.Timestamp):return x.isoformat(sep=' ')
    return x
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def pp(x):return f'{x*100:.2f}%'
def row(f,**keys):
    mask=pd.Series(True,index=f.index)
    for k,v in keys.items():mask &= f[k].eq(v)
    g=f.loc[mask];assert len(g)==1,(keys,len(g))
    return g.iloc[0]

def main():
    labels=pd.read_parquet(V/'evaluation/labels_evaluated.parquet')
    scored=labels.loc[labels.as_of_time.ge(pd.Timestamp('2026-10-09'))&labels.as_of_time.lt(pd.Timestamp('2026-10-22'))&labels.p_v4_d3.notna()].copy()
    primary=scored.loc[scored.y_d3.notna()].copy()
    assert len(scored)==8866 and len(primary)==8717
    assert primary.y_d0.notna().all()
    assert primary.p_v3_d0.notna().all() and primary.p_v3_d3.notna().all()
    metrics={};deciles={}
    for h in [0,3]:
        metrics[str(h)]={};deciles[str(h)]={}
        for model in ['v4','v3']:
            s=SortedMetrics(primary[f'y_d{h}'].to_numpy(float),primary[f'p_{model}_d{h}'].to_numpy(float))
            metrics[str(h)][model]=s.compute();deciles[str(h)][model]=s.deciles()
    hi=deciles['0']['v4'][0];lo=deciles['0']['v4'][-1]
    hi3=deciles['0']['v3'][0]
    target=scored.loc[scored.p_v4_d3.lt(.5)]
    target_known=target.loc[target.y_d0.notna()]
    assert len(target)==3701 and len(target_known)==3656
    assert target_known.y_d3.notna().all()
    no0=int(target_known.y_d0.eq(0).sum());yes0=int(target_known.y_d0.eq(1).sum())
    no3=int(target_known.y_d3.eq(0).sum());yes3=int(target_known.y_d3.eq(1).sum())
    assert no0==3143 and yes0==513 and no3==2488 and yes3==1168
    weekly=[]
    for week,g in primary.groupby('offer_week'):
        vals={m:SortedMetrics(g.y_d0.to_numpy(float),g[f'p_{m}_d0'].to_numpy(float)).compute() for m in ['v4','v3']}
        weekly.append({'week_monday':week,'event_date_min':g.as_of_time.min().date().isoformat(),
          'event_date_max':g.as_of_time.max().date().isoformat(),'n_events':len(g),
          'v4_top10_actual_rate':vals['v4']['top10_precision'],'v3_top10_actual_rate':vals['v3']['top10_precision'],
          'difference_pp':100*(vals['v4']['top10_precision']-vals['v3']['top10_precision'])})
    examples=[
      {'id':'ranking_extremes','headline':'高低意愿名单能明显分开，但不是逐人保证',

       'top10':hi,'bottom10':lo,'bottom10_nonapply_rate':1-lo['actual_rate'],
       'business_copy':f"最高10%名单实际当天提单率{pp(hi['actual_rate'])}，最低10%为{pp(lo['actual_rate'])}；最低档仍有{lo['positive_weight']:.1f}次提单权重，并非都不提单。",
       'count_wording':'每档871.7是精确10%预算和同分边界的事件权重；不是871.7名真实用户，782.0/46.0同样是提单权重，不可四舍五入写成用户人数。',
       'supports':'可用于排序名单、优先识别自然提单意愿高低','does_not_prove':'不保证每位低分用户都不提单，也未证明优惠有效'},
      {'id':'probability_calibration','headline':'总体概率接近实际，局部仍有偏差',

       'supports':'相同样本和预算下高意愿名单略好','does_not_prove':'不是新增14.3名借款用户，不等于营销增量，也不能套用原10,695事件比较的置信区间到本8,717事件重算'},
      {'id':'negative_list','headline':'筛出低pD3名单后，当天不提单命中率约86%，仍会误选',

       'source':'Existing observational design; no treatment assignment recorded'}]
    original_overall=pd.read_csv(V/'evaluation/overall.csv')
    original_deciles=pd.read_csv(V/'evaluation/deciles.csv')
    supplementary={'label':'原D0独立主检验口径，10/9—24，不能与上面8,717主样本混算',
      'overall':row(original_overall,cohort='main',horizon=0,model='v4').to_dict(),
      'deciles':original_deciles.loc[original_deciles.cohort.eq('main')&original_deciles.horizon.eq(0)&original_deciles.model.eq('v4')&original_deciles.decile.isin([1,10])].to_dict('records'),
      'caveat':'含10月24日风险字段缺失敏感性问题，仅作原报告数值追溯'}
    risk_meta=json.loads((R/'quality.json').read_text())
    result={'meta':{'offline_only':True,'database_queried':False,'model_retrained':False,'money_refreshed':False,
       'financial_snapshot_belize':risk_meta['snapshot']['snapshot_belize'],
       'primary_events':len(primary),'primary_users':primary.user_id.nunique(),'primary_dates':'2026-10-09 through 2026-10-21 America/Belize',
       'primary_rule':'V4 D3 probability available and frozen D3 outcome known; D0 outcomes also all known',
       'scored_before_unknown_exclusion':len(scored),'unknown_d3_events':len(scored)-len(primary),
       'D0_definition':'from T0 offer time through end of that Belize calendar day',
       'D3_definition':'D0 through D3 calendar-day cumulative; exclusive boundary offer date + 4 days 00:00, not rolling 72h',
       'counts_are_events':'Repeat users retained; fractional decile counts are weights, not people',
       'tie_rule':'Exact-score ties share rank-boundary inclusion weight equally; no outcome or ID breaks ties',
       'evidence_type':'retrospective replay, not prospective original scoring with proven score-availability timestamps'},
      'business_examples':examples,'primary_metrics':metrics,'primary_deciles':deciles,
      'anonymous_cases':cases,'anonymous_case_caveat':'案例按预定义类别最早T0选择，仅解释模型可能出现的结果，不代表群体比例，也不代替总体检验；不输出业务用户ID或原获额单编号。',
      'evidence_chain':chain,
      'evidence_chain_population_note':'历史盈利用1月1日—10月24日全体严格T0事件及既有10月25日回款快照，模型名单效果用10月9—21日冻结评分/反馈；两者提供不同证据，不能拼成某优惠策略的已实现金融收益。',
      'original_D0_independent_supplement':supplementary,
      'sources':{str(p.relative_to(W.parent)):sha(p) for p in [V/'evaluation/labels_evaluated.parquet',V/'evaluation/overall.csv',V/'evaluation/deciles.csv',V/'evaluation/calibration.csv',V/'evaluation/weekly.csv',V/'evaluation/pairwise.csv',R/'cells.csv',R/'prediction_profiles.csv',R/'validation_mapping.parquet']}}
    out=W/'business_examples.json';out.write_text(json.dumps(clean(result),ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    assert all('user_id' not in x and 'event_id' not in x and 'serial_id' not in x for x in cases)
    print(json.dumps({'output':str(out),'primary_events':len(primary),'anonymous_cases':len(cases),'top10_d0_actual':hi['actual_rate'],'bottom10_d0_actual':lo['actual_rate'],'weighted_extra_hits_v4_vs_v3':hi['positive_weight']-hi3['positive_weight']},ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':main()

def pct(v,n=2): return '—' if v is None else f'{v*100:.{n}f}%'
def num(v): return f'{v:,}'
def table(head, rows):
 return '<div class="scroll"><table><thead><tr>'+''.join('<th>'+str(x)+'</th>' for x in head)+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+''.join('<tr>'+''.join('<td>'+str(x)+'</td>' for x in r)+'</tr>' for r in rows)+'</tbody></table></div>'
def fold(title,body): return f'<details><summary>{title}</summary>{body}</details>'
def note(t): return '<p class="note">'+t+'</p>'
def call(t): return '<div class="callout">'+t+'</div>'
def section(id,title,body):return f'<section id="{id}"><h2>{title}</h2>{body}</section>'
# Independent bars avoid line/label collisions and work offline; labels reserve a fixed right column.
CHARTS=[]
def bars(id,title,rows,unit='%',maxval=100):
 # rows=(label,value,color,optional display); no shared text area with mark geometry.
 width=1060; left=310; span=510; right=width-35; top=48; step=49; height=top+step*len(rows)+28
 s=[f'<svg id="{id}" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" role="img" aria-label="{e(title)}"><title>{e(title)}</title>']
 for i,(label,value,color,display) in enumerate(rows):
  y=top+i*step; w=max(0,min(span,value/maxval*span))
  s+=[f'<text x="{left-18}" y="{y+5}" text-anchor="end" fill="#cbd5e1">{e(label)}</text>',f'<rect x="{left}" y="{y-13}" width="{span}" height="24" rx="4" fill="#202d43"/>',f'<rect x="{left}" y="{y-13}" width="{w:.3f}" height="24" rx="4" fill="{color}"/>',f'<text x="{right}" y="{y+5}" text-anchor="end" fill="#f8fafc" font-weight="700">{e(display or f"{value:.2f}{unit}")}</text>']
 s+=['</svg>'];CHARTS.append({'id':id,'rows':len(rows),'right_label_column':right,'bar_end_max':left+span,'height':height,'viewbox_width':width})
 return '<figure><h3>'+title+'</h3>'+''.join(s)+'</figure>'

原验证复原/code/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/build_features_replay.py
"""Frozen T0 V3/V4 feature replay, with no writes to the original versions.

Run with system python3 (numpy 2 / pandas 2), not the Anaconda numpy 1 runtime.
Only source CSV/parquet/pickle inputs are read. The SQL/extraction stage is separate.
The original pure function bodies are compiled from their AST: their path setup,
directory creation, DB imports and executable pipeline entrypoints never run.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, ast, hashlib, json, multiprocessing, time
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ORIGINAL = Path('/Users/wangchuanliang/Documents/Codex/2026-09-21/ji-x/outputs')
V1, V2, V3, V4 = [ORIGINAL / ('T0提单时点预测模型_V' + str(i)) for i in [1,2,3,4]]
PAGES = ['newpage','amount','contract','term','confirm','plan','purpose','coupon_back','coupon_confirm','coupon_page','repayment_page']
BEH = ['events'] + PAGES + ['duration','apply_duration']
_CTX = None

def pure_module(path, constants=None):
    """Load definitions only, never import original common/db or execute its main."""
    tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text())
    keep = [n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.ClassDef))]
    ns = {'np': np, 'pd': pd, 'lru_cache': lru_cache}
    ns.update(constants or {})
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=keep, type_ignores=[]), str(path), 'exec'), ns)
    return ns

V1F = pure_module(V1/'model.py', {'CORE':[], 'RISK':[], 'CATEGORIES':[],
    'HORIZONS':[0,3,7,15], 'END_DAYS':[1,4,8,16], 'START_DAYS':[0,1,4,8]})
# The frozen constants are declarative literals; no old module imports execute.
for node in ast.parse((V1/'model.py').read_text()).body:
    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(z,ast.Name) and z.id in ['CORE','RISK','CATEGORIES'] for z in node.targets):
        exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[node],type_ignores=[]),str(V1/'model.py'),'exec'),V1F)
V2F = pure_module(V2/'build_features.py', {'WINDOWS':[1,3,7,14,30,60,90], 'BEH':BEH, 'DAY_NS':86400*10**9})
V3F = pure_module(V3/'history_features.py', {'SPECS':{}, 'DAY':86400.,
    'SCOPES':['life','last3','last5','30d','90d','180d'],
    'SCOPE_CN':{'life':'全可观察历史','last3':'最近3次资格/事件','last5':'最近5次资格/事件','30d':'最近30天','90d':'最近90天','180d':'最近180天'}})
V4F = pure_module(V4/'product_features.py', {'ATTRS':['fee','daily','days','terms'],
    'ATTR_CN':{'fee':'息费率','daily':'日息','days':'账期天数','terms':'期次'}, 'SPECS':{}})

def digest(p):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(p).open('rb') as f:
        for z in iter(lambda:f.read(1048576),b''):h.update(z)
    return h.hexdigest()

def read_frame(path, **kwargs):
    path=Path(path)
    if path.suffix=='.parquet':return pd.read_parquet(path)
    if '.pkl' in path.name:return pd.read_pickle(path,compression='infer')
    return pd.read_csv(path,**kwargs)

def times(frame, cols):
    frame=frame.copy()
    for c in cols:
        if c in frame:frame[c]=pd.to_datetime(frame[c],format='mixed',errors='coerce')
    return frame

def require(frame, cols, name):
    absent=sorted(set(cols)-set(frame.columns))
    if absent:raise ValueError(name+' is structurally incomplete: '+','.join(absent))

def enrich_histories(orders, credits, payments):
    """Exact V2 load_histories transformations, returning in memory only."""
    orders=times(orders,['reg_date','apply_time','apply_date','remit_time','remit_date','repaid_time','repaid_date','due_date'])
    credits=times(credits,['credit_time'])
    payments=times(payments,['payin_date'])
    require(orders,['user_id','serial_id','reg_date','apply_amt','remit_amt','pre_amt','post_amt','loan_day',
        'total_term','is_remit','is_repaid','loan_status_code','remit_succ_rnb','product_no','loan_type'], 'orders')
    require(credits,['user_id','serial_id','credit_time','credit_amt','is_pass1','risk_product'],'credits')
    require(payments,['user_id','serial_id','payin_date','payin_time','amount','source_rows'],'payments')
    if orders.duplicated(['user_id','serial_id']).any():raise ValueError('Duplicate order snapshots must be resolved by extractor')
    if orders.apply_time.isna().any():raise ValueError('Order apply_time missing; original merge_asof contract cannot reconstruct these rows')
    orders=orders.sort_values(['user_id','apply_time','serial_id']).reset_index(drop=True)
    credits=credits.sort_values(['user_id','credit_time','serial_id']).reset_index(drop=True)
    if credits.credit_time.isna().any():raise ValueError('Missing historical credit timestamp')
    unix=pd.to_numeric(payments.payin_time,errors='coerce');seconds=unix.where(unix<10**11,unix/1000)
    payments['payment_time']=pd.to_datetime(seconds,unit='s',utc=True,errors='coerce').dt.tz_convert('America/Belize').dt.tz_localize(None)
    payments['timestamp_valid']=(payments.payment_time.dt.normalize()==payments.payin_date)&(seconds>946684800)
    payments=payments.sort_values(['user_id','payment_time','serial_id']).reset_index(drop=True)
    approved=credits.loc[credits.is_pass1.eq(1),['user_id','serial_id','credit_time','credit_amt']].sort_values('credit_time')
    joined=pd.merge_asof(orders[['user_id','serial_id','apply_time']].assign(original_row=np.arange(len(orders))).sort_values('apply_time'),
        approved,left_on='apply_time',right_on='credit_time',by=['user_id','serial_id'],direction='backward')
    orders['credit_amt']=joined.sort_values('original_row').credit_amt.to_numpy()
    orders['utilization']=(orders.apply_amt/orders.credit_amt.where(orders.credit_amt>0)).where(orders.apply_amt>0)
    orders['fee_rate']=(orders.pre_amt+orders.post_amt)/orders.remit_amt.where(orders.remit_amt>0)
    orders['daily_rate']=orders.fee_rate/orders.loan_day.where(orders.loan_day>0)
    known=orders.loc[orders.repaid_time.gt(pd.Timestamp('2000-01-01'))&((orders.is_repaid==1)|(orders.loan_status_code==7)),['user_id','serial_id','repaid_time','repaid_date']]
    ledger=payments.loc[payments.timestamp_valid].merge(known,on=['user_id','serial_id'],how='inner',validate='many_to_one')
    ledger=ledger.loc[ledger.payment_time.le(ledger.repaid_time)]
    aggregate=ledger.groupby('serial_id').agg(flow_settle_time=('payment_time','max'),settle_cash=('amount','sum'),
        settle_payment_n=('source_rows','sum'),settle_payment_days=('payin_date','nunique'))
    orders=orders.merge(aggregate,left_on='serial_id',right_index=True,how='left',validate='one_to_one')
    bad=orders.flow_settle_time.dt.normalize().ne(orders.repaid_date)
    orders.loc[bad,'flow_settle_time']=pd.NaT
    orders.loc[bad,['settle_cash','settle_payment_n','settle_payment_days']]=np.nan
    orders['early_days']=(orders.due_date-orders.flow_settle_time.dt.normalize()).dt.days
    orders['apply_settle_days']=(orders.flow_settle_time-orders.apply_time).dt.total_seconds()/86400
    orders['remit_settle_days']=(orders.flow_settle_time-orders.remit_time).dt.total_seconds()/86400
    orders['settle_cash_remit_ratio']=orders.settle_cash/orders.remit_amt.where(orders.remit_amt>0)
    return orders,credits,payments

def old_monthly_profiles(events, profile_daily):
    """V1's month-lag prior-contract profiles; never current product_info."""
    p=times(profile_daily,['profile_date'])
    require(p,['profile_date','product_no','orders','remit_sum','fee_sum','day_sum','day_n','term_sum','term_n'],'profiles')
    rows=[]
    for month in sorted(events.vir_date.dt.to_period('M').dt.to_timestamp().unique()):
        g=p.loc[p.profile_date<pd.Timestamp(month)].groupby('product_no').sum(numeric_only=True)
        g['fee_rate']=g.fee_sum/g.remit_sum;g['loan_days']=g.day_sum/g.day_n;g['terms']=g.term_sum/g.term_n
        g['daily_rate']=g.fee_rate/g.loan_days
        em=events.loc[events.vir_date.dt.to_period('M').dt.to_timestamp().eq(month)]
        for e in em.itertuples():
            tokens=[x.strip() for x in str(e.risk_product).split(',') if x.strip() and x!='nan']
            valid=g.loc[g.index.intersection(tokens)]
            rows.append({'event_id':e.event_id,'current_product_profile_match_n':len(valid),
                'current_history_min_fee_rate':valid.fee_rate.min() if len(valid) else np.nan,
                'current_history_min_daily_rate':valid.daily_rate.min() if len(valid) else np.nan,
                'current_history_max_loan_days':valid.loan_days.max() if len(valid) else np.nan,
                'current_history_max_terms':valid.terms.max() if len(valid) else np.nan})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)

def prepare_base(events, behavior_v1, profile_daily):
    events=times(events,['as_of_time','vir_date','first_apply_time','followup_end','next_t0_time','data_cutoff_ts'])
    require(events,['event_id','user_id','as_of_time','vir_date','settled_serial_id','credit_serial_id',
        'risk_product','vir_version','previous_product_no','current_total_term'], 'event_features')
    if events.event_id.duplicated().any():raise ValueError('Event IDs not unique')
    prof=old_monthly_profiles(events,profile_daily)
    drop=[c for c in prof if c!='event_id' and c in events]
    base=events.drop(columns=drop).merge(prof,on='event_id',how='left',validate='one_to_one')
    require(behavior_v1,['event_id','beh_events_7d','beh_active_days_7d','beh_events_1d','beh_events_3d','beh_newpage_7d',
        'beh_amount_7d','beh_plan_7d','beh_term_7d','beh_confirm_7d','beh_duration_7d','days_since_active'],'behavior_v1')
    if set(base.event_id)!=set(behavior_v1.event_id):raise ValueError('V1 rolling7-day behavior must cover every event, including zero-activity events')
    base=base.drop(columns=[c for c in behavior_v1 if c!='event_id' and c in base]).merge(behavior_v1,on='event_id',how='left',validate='one_to_one')
    base['beh_source_available']=base.beh_events_7d.notna().astype(int)
    prepared=V1F['prepare'](base)
    needed=[n for n in V1F['CORE']+V1F['RISK'] if not n.startswith('interval_')]+V1F['CATEGORIES']
    require(prepared,needed,'prepared V1 core')
    return prepared

def product_histories(credits, offers, orders):
    c=credits.loc[credits.is_pass1.eq(1)].sort_values(['user_id','credit_time','serial_id'])
    if c.duplicated(['user_id','serial_id']).any():raise ValueError('Repeated approved credit snapshots: need timestamp-aware source resolution')
    eligible=offers.loc[offers.loan_type_code.eq(2)&(offers.has_type2.eq(1)|offers.has_type3.eq(1)),['user_id','serial_id']].drop_duplicates()
    c=c.merge(eligible,on=['user_id','serial_id'],how='inner',validate='one_to_one')
    c=c.merge(orders[['user_id','serial_id','apply_time','product_no']],on=['user_id','serial_id'],how='left',validate='one_to_one')
    groups={}
    for uid,g in c.groupby('user_id',sort=False):
        g=g.sort_values(['credit_time','serial_id'])
        batch=(g.credit_time.diff().gt(pd.Timedelta(seconds=20))|g.credit_time.diff().isna()).cumsum()
        starts=g.groupby(batch).credit_time.min();final=g.loc[~batch.duplicated(keep='last')].copy()
        final['batch_start']=batch.loc[final.index].map(starts)
        recs=[(r.credit_time,V4F['tokens'](r.risk_product),int(r.serial_id),r.apply_time,str(r.product_no) if pd.notna(r.product_no) else '') for r in final.itertuples()]
        groups[int(uid)]=(final.batch_start.to_numpy('datetime64[ns]'),final.credit_time.to_numpy('datetime64[ns]'),recs)
    return groups

def one_row(i):
    c=_CTX;e=c['events'][i];uid=int(e.user_id)
    o,cr,p,d,v=[g.get(uid,z) for g,z in zip(c['groups'],c['empty'])]
    row=V2F['loan_features'](o,p,cr,e.as_of_time,e.settled_serial_id,e.current_credit_amt,e.previous_credit_amt)
    eq=o.loc[o.repaid_time.eq(e.as_of_time)&o.is_remit.eq(1)&o.remit_amt.gt(0)&o.remit_time.le(e.as_of_time)&((o.is_repaid==1)|(o.loan_status_code==7)),'serial_id'].tolist()
    row.update(V2F['behavior_features'](d,c['prefix'].loc[e.event_id].to_dict(),p,e.as_of_time,o.reg_date.min(),eq))
    row['current_total_term_known']=e.current_total_term if e.current_total_term>0 else np.nan
    for new,current,previous in [('term','current_history_max_terms','previous_total_term'),('days','current_history_max_loan_days','previous_loan_day'),
        ('fee','current_history_min_fee_rate','previous_fee_rate'),('daily','current_history_min_daily_rate','previous_daily_rate')]:
        row['current_'+new+'_to_previous']=V2F['safe'](getattr(e,current),getattr(e,previous))
    change=e.current_credit_amt-e.previous_credit_amt
    row['current_credit_change_abs_band']=int(np.digitize(abs(change),[500,1000,2000,3000])) if np.isfinite(change) else np.nan
    ratio=V2F['safe'](abs(change),e.previous_credit_amt)
    row['current_credit_change_relative_band']=int(np.digitize(ratio,[.1,.25,.5,1])) if np.isfinite(ratio) else np.nan
    for metric,tol in [('fee_rate',.01),('daily_rate',.0005)]:
        value=np.round(getattr(e,metric+'_change'),12)
        row['current_'+metric+'_direction_tolerance']=float(np.sign(value)) if np.isfinite(value) and abs(value)>=tol else (0 if np.isfinite(value) else np.nan)
    if set(row)!=set(c['v2_names']):raise AssertionError('Frozen V2 feature schema changed')
    hist,audit=V3F['snapshot'](v,o,e.as_of_time,e.event_id,e.current_credit_amt,e.previous_utilization,e.successful_loan_count,e.credit_direction)
    if set(hist)!=set(c['v3_new_names']):raise AssertionError('Frozen V3 feature schema changed')
    g=c['products'].get(uid)
    if g:
        starts,ts,recs=g;k=np.searchsorted(ts,e.as_of_time.to_datetime64(),side='right')
        current_start=starts[k-1] if k and ts[k-1]==e.as_of_time.to_datetime64() else e.as_of_time.to_datetime64()
        h=np.searchsorted(ts,current_start,side='left');ph=recs[:h]
    else:ph=[]
    prod,paudit=V4F['snapshot'](ph,e.as_of_time,e.risk_product,c['profiles'],
        [e.previous_fee_rate,e.previous_daily_rate,e.previous_loan_day,e.previous_total_term],e.successful_loan_count)
    if set(prod)!=set(c['product_names']) or paudit['future_history_used']:raise AssertionError('Frozen V4 product schema/time changed')
    allvals={**row,**hist,**prod}
    # V3 base category values use its frozen training maps, not fresh levels.
    for n in c['base_names']:
        if n.startswith('interval_'):allvals[n]=0
        elif n in c['maps']:allvals[n]=c['maps'][n].get(str(getattr(e,n)) ,np.nan)
        else:allvals[n]=getattr(e,n)
    v3=np.array([allvals[n] for n in c['v3_names']],dtype='float32')
    v4=np.array([allvals[n] for n in c['v4_names']],dtype='float32')
    v3[np.isinf(v3)]=np.nan;v4[np.isinf(v4)]=np.nan
    audit.update(event_id=int(e.event_id),life_funded_n=row['life_funded_n'],cached_funded_n=e.successful_loan_count,
        prefix_last_active=str(c['prefix'].loc[e.event_id,'last_active_time']))
    return v3,v4,audit

def chunk(bounds):
    a,b=bounds;vals=[one_row(i) for i in range(a,b)]
    return a,b,np.stack([x[0] for x in vals]),np.stack([x[1] for x in vals]),[x[2] for x in vals]

def build_replay(inputs, output_dir, workers=4, chunk_size=500):
    """inputs: dict of named DataFrames; returns matrix paths and audit dictionary."""
    global _CTX
    at=time.time();out=Path(output_dir).resolve()
    if ORIGINAL.resolve() in out.parents:raise ValueError('Never write into original versions')
    out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    orders,credits,payments=enrich_histories(inputs['orders'],inputs['credits'],inputs['payments'])
    offers=times(inputs['history_offers'],['credit_time','vir_date'])
    require(offers,['user_id','serial_id','credit_time','vir_date','credit_amt','loan_type_code','has_type2','has_type3','type_distinct_n','export_id'],'history_offers')
    if not offers.type_distinct_n.le(1).all():raise ValueError('Ambiguous historical recycle types')
    daily=times(inputs['daily_behavior'],['create_date','last_active_time'])
    require(daily,['user_id','create_date','last_active_time']+BEH,'daily_behavior')
    if daily.duplicated(['user_id','create_date']).any():raise ValueError('Daily source rows duplicated; frozen/incremental overlap must be resolved')
    bad=daily.last_active_time.ge(daily.create_date+pd.Timedelta(days=1))|daily.last_active_time.lt(daily.create_date)
    bad_n=int(bad.sum());daily=daily.loc[~bad].sort_values(['user_id','create_date'])
    prefix=times(inputs['prefix_behavior'],['last_active_time']).set_index('event_id',verify_integrity=True)
    require(prefix,['last_active_time']+BEH,'prefix_behavior')
    profile=inputs['product_profiles_daily'].copy();profile['product_no']=profile.product_no.astype(str)
    base=prepare_base(inputs['events'],inputs['behavior_v1'],profile)
    if set(prefix.index)!=set(base.event_id):raise ValueError('Prefix must contain every event exactly once')
    aligned=prefix.reindex(base.event_id)
    if not (aligned.last_active_time.isna().to_numpy()|(aligned.last_active_time.to_numpy()<base.as_of_time.to_numpy())).all():raise ValueError('Future behavior in current-day prefix')
    v2_names=json.loads((V2/'datasets/lifecycle_feature_names.json').read_text())
    v3_names=json.loads((V3/'datasets/model_feature_names.json').read_text())
    v3_life=json.loads((V3/'datasets/lifecycle_feature_names.json').read_text())
    product_names=json.loads((V4/'datasets/product_feature_names.json').read_text())
    v4_names=json.loads((V4/'datasets/numeric_feature_names.json').read_text())
    base_names=[n for n in v3_names if n not in set(v3_life)]
    require(base,[n for n in base_names if not n.startswith('interval_')],'frozen base input')
    groups=[{int(u):g for u,g in f.groupby('user_id',sort=False)} for f in [orders,credits,payments,daily,offers]]
    _CTX={'events':tuple(base.itertuples()),'groups':groups,'empty':[f.iloc[0:0] for f in [orders,credits,payments,daily,offers]],
        'prefix':prefix,'products':product_histories(credits,offers,orders),'profiles':V4F['Profiles'](profile),
        'v2_names':v2_names,'v3_new_names':v3_life[len(v2_names):], 'v3_names':v3_names,'v4_names':v4_names,
        'base_names':base_names,'product_names':product_names,'maps':json.loads((V3/'datasets/categorical_mappings.json').read_text())}
    a3=np.lib.format.open_memmap(out/'v3_model_features.npy',mode='w+',dtype='float32',shape=(len(base),len(v3_names)))
    a4=np.lib.format.open_memmap(out/'v4_numeric_features.npy',mode='w+',dtype='float32',shape=(len(base),len(v4_names)))
    bounds=[(a,min(a+chunk_size,len(base))) for a in range(0,len(base),chunk_size)];audits=[]
    if workers>1:
        with multiprocessing.get_context('fork').Pool(workers) as pool:
            for start,stop,x3,x4,au in pool.imap(chunk,bounds,chunksize=1):
                a3[start:stop]=x3;a4[start:stop]=x4;audits.extend(au);a3.flush();a4.flush()
                print('REPLAY_FEATURES',stop,len(base),'seconds',round(time.time()-at),flush=True)
    else:
        for start,stop,x3,x4,au in map(chunk,bounds):
            a3[start:stop]=x3;a4[start:stop]=x4;audits.extend(au);a3.flush();a4.flush()
            print('REPLAY_FEATURES',stop,len(base),'seconds',round(time.time()-at),flush=True)
    base.to_pickle(out/'base_with_labels.pkl.gz',compression='gzip')
    for file,names in [('v3_feature_names.json',v3_names),('v4_numeric_names.json',v4_names)]:
        (out/file).write_text(json.dumps(names,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    audit_df=pd.DataFrame(audits);audit_df.to_csv(out/'event_feature_audits.csv.gz',index=False,compression='gzip')
    audit={'events':len(base),'users':int(base.user_id.nunique()),'v3_columns':len(v3_names),'v4_numeric_columns':len(v4_names),
        'invalid_daily_timestamp_groups_removed':bad_n,'invalid_payment_timestamp_rows':int((~payments.timestamp_valid).sum()),
        'funded_count_mismatch_events':int(audit_df.life_funded_n.ne(audit_df.cached_funded_n).sum()),
        'seconds':round(time.time()-at,1),'no_old_directory_writes':True,'structural_missing_feature_fill':False,
        'v3_missing_share':float(np.isnan(a3).mean()),'v4_missing_share':float(np.isnan(a4).mean()),
        'code_fingerprints':{str(p):digest(p) for p in [V1/'model.py',V2/'build_features.py',V3/'history_features.py',V4/'product_features.py',Path(__file__)]},
        'limitations':['Risk scores have no availability timestamp, same as frozen model.',
            'Recycle-type and contract fields may be mutable; current extracts are retrospective reconstruction, not contemporaneously stored snapshots.',
            'Daily behavior available from 2024-01-01; pre-2024 registrations are explicitly left-censored.',
            'Missing numeric values are genuine frozen formulas (no history/zero denominator), not a missing-column workaround.']}
    (out/'feature_replay_audit.json').write_text(json.dumps(audit,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    return audit

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--manifest',required=True);p.add_argument('--output-dir',required=True)
    p.add_argument('--workers',type=int,default=4);p.add_argument('--chunk-size',type=int,default=500);args=p.parse_args()
    manifest=json.loads(Path(args.manifest).read_text());paths=manifest.get('sources',manifest)
    inputs={}
    for name in ['events','orders','credits','payments','daily_behavior','prefix_behavior','history_offers','product_profiles_daily','behavior_v1']:
        path=paths[name]
        if isinstance(path,list):inputs[name]=pd.concat([read_frame(x,dtype={'product_no':str,'risk_product':str,'loan_type':str,'vir_version':str,'previous_product_no':str}) for x in path],ignore_index=True)
        else:inputs[name]=read_frame(path,dtype={'product_no':str,'risk_product':str,'loan_type':str,'vir_version':str,'previous_product_no':str})
    print(json.dumps(build_replay(inputs,args.output_dir,args.workers,args.chunk_size),ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':main()
原验证复原/code/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/score_and_assemble.py
"""Score frozen T0 V3/V4 snapshots, then rebuild observation labels locally.

No DB access, training, calibration fitting or writes to original artifacts.
Run with system python3 / numpy 2. Frozen pure scoring function bodies are
compiled from AST so importing old common modules cannot create directories.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import ast
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
OLD = Path("/Users/wangchuanliang/Documents/Codex/2026-09-21/ji-x/outputs")
V1, V3, V4 = [OLD / f"T0提单时点预测模型_V{i}" for i in [1, 3, 4]]
HORIZONS, START_DAYS, END_DAYS = [0, 3, 7, 15], [0, 1, 4, 8], [1, 4, 8, 16]
CUTOFF = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-25 00:00:00")
sys.path.insert(0, str(V1 / "runtime"))
import joblib


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def definitions(path: Path, names: list[str], globals_: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())
    selected = [node for node in tree.body if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)) and node.name in names]
    if {node.name for node in selected} != set(names):
        raise ValueError(f"Missing frozen scoring functions in {path}")
    namespace = {"np": np, "pd": pd, "HORIZONS": HORIZONS, "START_DAYS": START_DAYS, "END_DAYS": END_DAYS}
    namespace.update(globals_)
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=selected, type_ignores=[]), str(path), "exec"), namespace)
    return namespace


def scoring_functions():
    tokens = definitions(V4 / "product_features.py", ["tokens"], {})["tokens"]
    v4 = definitions(V4 / "model_v4.py", ["encode", "apply_calibration", "predict"],
                     {"tokens": tokens, "CATS": ["vir_version", "previous_product_no", "risk_product"]})
    v3 = definitions(V3 / "train_v3.py", ["predict"], {})
    return v3["predict"], v4["predict"]


def assert_probabilities(p: np.ndarray, n: int, name: str) -> None:
    if p.shape != (n, 4) or not np.isfinite(p).all() or (p < 0).any() or (p > 1).any():
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid probability matrix: {name} {p.shape}, expected ({n},4)")
    if (np.diff(p, axis=1) < -1e-10).any():
        raise ValueError(f"Cumulative probability monotonicity violated: {name}")


def timestamps(frame: pd.DataFrame, columns: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    frame = frame.copy()
    for c in columns:
        if c in frame:
            valid = frame[c].notna()
            frame[c] = pd.to_datetime(frame[c], format="mixed", errors="coerce")
            if (valid & frame[c].isna()).any():
                raise ValueError(f"Unparseable timestamps in {c}")
    return frame


def id_check(frame: pd.DataFrame, name: str) -> None:
    if frame.event_id.isna().any() or frame.event_id.duplicated().any():
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: event ids must be present and unique")
    if frame.user_id.isna().any() or frame.as_of_time.isna().any():
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: user/event time missing")


def check_identity(a: pd.DataFrame, b: pd.DataFrame, name: str, require_same_order: bool = True) -> None:
    if require_same_order and not np.array_equal(a.event_id.to_numpy(), b.event_id.to_numpy()):
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: event-id order changed")
    aligned = b.set_index("event_id").reindex(a.event_id)
    if aligned.user_id.isna().any():
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: event id missing")
    if not np.array_equal(a.user_id.to_numpy(), aligned.user_id.to_numpy()):
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: event/user identity mismatch")
    if not np.array_equal(a.as_of_time.to_numpy(), aligned.as_of_time.to_numpy()):
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: event/as-of identity mismatch")


def old_september() -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, Any], pd.DataFrame]:
    base4_path = V4 / "datasets/base_with_labels.pkl.gz"
    base3_path = V3 / "datasets/base_with_labels.pkl.gz"
    base = timestamps(pd.read_pickle(base4_path, compression="gzip"), ["as_of_time", "vir_date"])
    b3 = timestamps(pd.read_pickle(base3_path, compression="gzip"), ["as_of_time", "vir_date"])
    id_check(base, "V4 old mother")
    id_check(b3, "V3 old mother")
    check_identity(base, b3, "V3 vs V4 old mothers")
    p4_path = V4 / "datasets/event_predictions.npy"
    p4 = np.load(p4_path, mmap_mode="r")
    assert_probabilities(p4, len(base), "V4 stored")
    columns = ["event_id", "user_id", "as_of_time"] + [f"p_apply_d{h}" for h in HORIZONS]
    p3_path = V3 / "T0提单时点预测模型_V3_全量预测结果.csv.gz"
    p4_csv_path = V4 / "T0提单时点预测模型_V4_全量预测结果.csv.gz"
    csv3 = timestamps(pd.read_csv(p3_path, usecols=columns), ["as_of_time"])
    csv4 = timestamps(pd.read_csv(p4_csv_path, usecols=columns), ["as_of_time"])
    id_check(csv3, "V3 stored prediction CSV")
    id_check(csv4, "V4 stored prediction CSV")
    check_identity(base, csv3, "V3 stored predictions")
    check_identity(base, csv4, "V4 stored prediction row order")
    np.testing.assert_allclose(p4, csv4[[f"p_apply_d{h}" for h in HORIZONS]].to_numpy(float), rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12)
    p3 = csv3[[f"p_apply_d{h}" for h in HORIZONS]].to_numpy(float)
    assert_probabilities(p3, len(base), "V3 stored")
    mask = (base.as_of_time >= pd.Timestamp("2026-09-22")) & (base.as_of_time < pd.Timestamp("2026-10-01"))
    selected = base.loc[mask].copy()
    for j, h in enumerate(HORIZONS):
        selected[f"p_v4_d{h}"] = p4[np.flatnonzero(mask), j]
        selected[f"p_v3_d{h}"] = p3[mask, j]
    selected["prediction_source"] = "original_saved_historical_predictions"
    selected["prediction_time_status"] = "historical snapshot prediction saved in original artifacts; not certified as online contemporaneous scoring"
    # Pull only frozen pre-T0 cycle flags needed for the subgroup labels.
    old_names = json.loads((V3 / "datasets/model_feature_names.json").read_text())
    old_x = np.load(V3 / "datasets/model_features.npy", mmap_mode="r")
    for c in ["cyc_ever_eligible", "cyc_ever_applied"]:
        if c not in old_names:
            raise ValueError(f"Missing frozen cycle-history feature {c}")
        selected[c] = old_x[np.flatnonzero(mask), old_names.index(c)]
    audit = {"old_mother_identity_and_order_equal": True, "v4_npy_equals_csv": True,
             "v3_saved_prediction_format": "CSV; original V3 did not save event_predictions.npy",
             "september_events": len(selected), "september_users": selected.user_id.nunique(),
             "sha256": {str(p): digest(p) for p in [base4_path, base3_path, p4_path, p3_path, p4_csv_path]}}
    return selected, audit, base


def score_october(features: Path) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, Any]]:
    base_path = features / "base_with_labels.pkl.gz"
    frame = timestamps(pd.read_pickle(base_path, compression="gzip"), ["as_of_time", "vir_date", "first_apply_time", "next_t0_time"])
    id_check(frame, "October replay snapshots")
    if not ((frame.as_of_time >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-01")) & (frame.as_of_time < CUTOFF)).all():
        raise ValueError("October replay frame outside locked dates")
    x3_path, x4_path = features / "v3_model_features.npy", features / "v4_numeric_features.npy"
    names3_path, names4_path = features / "v3_feature_names.json", features / "v4_numeric_names.json"
    x3, x4 = np.load(x3_path, mmap_mode="r"), np.load(x4_path, mmap_mode="r")
    names3, names4 = json.loads(names3_path.read_text()), json.loads(names4_path.read_text())
    expected3 = json.loads((V3 / "datasets/model_feature_names.json").read_text())
    expected4 = json.loads((V4 / "datasets/numeric_feature_names.json").read_text())
    if names3 != expected3 or names4 != expected4:
        raise ValueError("Feature names/order differ from frozen model contract")
    if x3.shape != (len(frame), len(names3)) or x4.shape != (len(frame), len(names4)):
        raise ValueError("Feature matrix rows or columns differ from snapshots")
    models = {"v3": V3 / "models/t0_application_timing_v3.joblib", "v4": V4 / "models/t0_application_timing_v4.joblib"}
    sha_before = {m: digest(p) for m, p in models.items()}
    bundle3, bundle4 = joblib.load(models["v3"]), joblib.load(models["v4"])
    predict3, predict4 = scoring_functions()
    if bundle4["numeric_names"] != names4:
        raise ValueError("V4 numeric names differ from saved bundle")
    p3, hazard3 = predict3(bundle3, x3)
    p4, hazard4 = predict4(bundle4, x4, frame)
    assert_probabilities(p3, len(frame), "V3 October replay")
    assert_probabilities(p4, len(frame), "V4 October replay")
    for j, h in enumerate(HORIZONS):
        frame[f"p_v3_d{h}"] = p3[:, j]
        frame[f"p_v4_d{h}"] = p4[:, j]
    for c in ["cyc_ever_eligible", "cyc_ever_applied"]:
        frame[c] = x3[:, names3.index(c)]
    frame["prediction_source"] = "frozen_model_retrospective_feature_replay"
    frame["prediction_time_status"] = "scored now using reconstructed historical snapshots; not original online predictions"
    sha_after = {m: digest(p) for m, p in models.items()}
    if sha_before != sha_after:
        raise AssertionError("Frozen model files changed")
    audit = {"october_events": len(frame), "october_users": frame.user_id.nunique(),
             "model_sha256_before": sha_before, "model_sha256_after": sha_after, "models_unchanged": True,
             "model_versions": {"v3": bundle3["version"], "v4": bundle4["version"]},
             "scoring_function_ast_only": True, "monotonic_predictions": True,
             "original_model_training_or_calibration_run": False,
             "input_sha256": {str(p): digest(p) for p in [base_path, x3_path, x4_path, names3_path, names4_path]},
             "score_code_sha256": {str(p): digest(p) for p in [V3 / "train_v3.py", V4 / "model_v4.py", V4 / "product_features.py"]}}
    return frame, audit


def rebuild_observation(events: pd.DataFrame, old_mother: pd.DataFrame, october: pd.DataFrame,
                        orders: pd.DataFrame, change_audit_path: Path | None = None) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Use original retained T0 IDs and all order apply times, never row numbers."""
    events = events.copy()
    keys = ["event_id", "user_id", "as_of_time"]
    mother = pd.concat([old_mother[keys], october[keys]], ignore_index=True)
    id_check(mother, "Combined original strict T0 mother")
    mother = mother.sort_values(["user_id", "as_of_time", "event_id"])
    if mother.duplicated(["user_id", "as_of_time"]).any():
        raise ValueError("Combined retained T0 mother has tied user/as_of_time; verify 20-second dedup")
    mother["next_t0_time"] = mother.groupby("user_id", sort=False).as_of_time.shift(-1)
    next_by_id = mother.set_index("event_id").next_t0_time
    cached_apply = pd.to_datetime(events.first_apply_time, errors="coerce") if "first_apply_time" in events else pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index)
    cached_sources = pd.concat([old_mother, october], ignore_index=True).set_index("event_id")
    def cached_column(name):
        # Keep original stored boundaries, including the old October 7 cutoff.
        # The original September labels can contain an October-support next T0
        # even though the saved formal mother itself stops at September 30.
        if name in events:
            return pd.to_datetime(events[name], errors="coerce")
        if name in cached_sources:
            return pd.to_datetime(events.event_id.map(cached_sources[name]), errors="coerce")
        return pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    cached_next = cached_column("next_t0_time")
    cached_cutoff = cached_column("data_cutoff_ts")
    cached_followup = cached_column("followup_end")
    events["next_t0_time"] = events.event_id.map(next_by_id)
    orders = timestamps(orders, ["apply_time"])
    required = ["user_id", "serial_id", "apply_time"]
    if not set(required).issubset(orders):
        raise ValueError(f"Order extract lacks {required}")
    if orders["user_id"].isna().any() or orders["apply_time"].isna().any():
        raise ValueError("Order extract has missing user_id or apply_time")
    if orders.duplicated(["user_id", "serial_id"]).any():
        raise ValueError("Order extract duplicates user/order identity")
    missing_users = set(events.user_id) - set(orders.user_id)
    if missing_users:
        raise ValueError(f"Historical order extract omits {len(missing_users)} scored users")
    followup = pd.Series(CUTOFF, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    for cap in [events.next_t0_time, events.as_of_time.dt.normalize() + pd.Timedelta(days=16)]:
        mask = cap.notna() & (cap < followup)
        followup.loc[mask] = cap.loc[mask]
    apply = pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    event_groups = events.groupby("user_id", sort=False).groups
    examined = 0
    for uid, og in orders.groupby("user_id", sort=False):
        if uid not in event_groups:
            continue
        ix = event_groups[uid]
        a = np.sort(og.apply_time.to_numpy("datetime64[ns]"))
        t = events.loc[ix, "as_of_time"].to_numpy("datetime64[ns]")
        where = np.searchsorted(a, t, side="left")
        valid = where < len(a)
        positions = np.flatnonzero(valid)
        if len(positions):
            candidate = a[where[positions]]
            before_end = candidate < followup.loc[ix].to_numpy("datetime64[ns]")[positions]
            chosen = np.asarray(ix)[positions[before_end]]
            apply.loc[chosen] = candidate[before_end]
        examined += len(ix)
    if examined != len(events):
        raise AssertionError("Not all event user histories were examined")
    equals = (cached_apply == apply) | (cached_apply.isna() & apply.isna())
    october_mask = events.as_of_time >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-01")
    changed = ~equals
    added = cached_apply.isna() & apply.notna()
    removed = cached_apply.notna() & apply.isna()
    moved = cached_apply.notna() & apply.notna() & (cached_apply != apply)
    next_cutoff_removed = removed & events.next_t0_time.notna() & (cached_apply >= events.next_t0_time)
    category = pd.Series("unchanged", index=events.index, dtype="string")
    category.loc[added] = "newly_observed_application"
    category.loc[next_cutoff_removed] = "old_application_removed_by_reconstructed_next_t0_boundary"
    category.loc[removed & ~next_cutoff_removed] = "old_application_removed_other_reason"
    category.loc[moved] = "application_time_changed_both_observed"
    category.loc[changed & category.eq("unchanged")] = "other_change"
    added_after_original = added & cached_cutoff.notna() & (apply >= cached_cutoff)
    added_before_original = added & cached_cutoff.notna() & (apply < cached_cutoff)
    next_boundary_changed = ~((cached_next == events.next_t0_time) | (cached_next.isna() & events.next_t0_time.isna()))
    change_rows = pd.DataFrame({
        "event_id": events.event_id, "event_time": events.as_of_time,
        "period": np.where(october_mask, "october", "september_saved"),
        "change_category": category, "cached_first_apply_time": cached_apply,
        "rebuilt_first_apply_time": apply, "cached_next_t0_time": cached_next,
        "rebuilt_next_t0_time": events.next_t0_time,
        "cached_data_cutoff_ts": cached_cutoff, "cached_followup_end": cached_followup,
        "rebuilt_followup_end": followup,
        "new_hit_at_or_after_original_cutoff": added_after_original,
        "new_hit_before_original_cutoff": added_before_original,
        "new_hit_at_or_after_20261007": added & (apply >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-07")),
        "next_t0_boundary_changed": next_boundary_changed,
    }).loc[changed].copy()
    if len(change_rows) != int(changed.sum()) or int(category.ne("unchanged").sum()) != int(changed.sum()):
        raise AssertionError("Changed-label audit categories do not reconcile")
    if change_audit_path is not None:
        change_audit_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # No user IDs, input contents, bank/phone details or other sensitive
        # attributes; this is an event/time audit and never HTML body content.
        change_rows.to_csv(change_audit_path, index=False)
    events["first_apply_time"] = apply
    events["followup_end"] = followup
    events["data_cutoff_ts"] = CUTOFF
    audit = {"combined_mother_events": len(mother), "orders": len(orders), "order_users": orders.user_id.nunique(),
